# The line bank: his answer to your move in a one-on-one, by how it landed,
# and the optional closing sentence after it (dialogue D2; format in
# src/world/folk/voice.rs). Same keys as players.txt:
# talk.reply.<move>.<good|bad|flat> and talk.close.<good|bad|flat>. Promise
# covers the depth chart, the ball, the collective, the agent, next year and
# you staying, so its lines never name which. Hand is the leadership council
# or time at home, so its lines fit both. Facts: name, full, coach, you, pos,
# team, friend. Lane 13c added about 8,300 versions and the talk.deflect block
# at the end (his words when he won't open up; more in talk_reveal.txt).
# None of these parts gets the subject, so no line names one, and no line
# claims family, an injury, a result or a date the world may contradict.
